# Commands · C · part 3

1,482 entries, ccc-claw to chatto-overview. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ccc-claw**: Start NanoClaw, the vendored zero-dependency REPL with persistent markdown sessions, model switching, skill hot-load, branching, search, compaction, export and metrics. Use when you want a lightweight persistent agent session outside the main chat. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-claw.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-cloudcli**: Open, inspect or start CloudCLI web sessions so Claude Code is reachable from a browser or phone. Use when the user asks for web or mobile access to a running session. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-cloudcli.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-complete**: Report a delegated task as finished: summary of changes, tests and commits, plus an optional tracker update. Use when a task delegated by an orchestrator or tracker is done. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-complete.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-compound**: Capture the patterns, corrections and decisions from the work just done into tasks/lessons.md, or review and prune existing lessons. Use after finishing a task or when the user says compound, lessons or retro. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-compound.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-context-budget**: Show how full the context window is, which components use the most of it, and when to save the session and compact. Use when the session feels slow, long or near its context limit. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-context-budget.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-cost**: Show what agent sessions cost today, yesterday and in total, broken down by day, agent or model, with a daily budget alert. Use when the user asks about spend, token usage or burn rate. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-cost.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-courseware**: Before displaying the catalog, run the update check and progress scan. ([rhpds/claude-code-courseware](https://github.com/rhpds/claude-code-courseware/blob/HEAD/lola/ccc/commands/ccc-courseware.md))
- **ccc-debug-session**: Run a structured debug session: gather context, rank hypotheses, test them without editing code, then fix with a regression test and record the lesson. Use when a bug's cause is unclear. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-debug-session.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-devfleet**: Plan a project into dependent missions and run parallel Claude Code agents in isolated worktrees through the DevFleet MCP server, then read each mission's report. Use when the user wants DevFleet orchestration. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-devfleet.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-docs**: Answer a library, framework or API question from its current documentation via Context7, with code snippets. Use when the user asks how to use a library or wants docs newer than training data. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-docs.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-eval**: Define, run, and report evals for a feature before and during implementation, tracking capability and regression pass rates in .claude/evals/. Use when starting eval-driven development or checking whether a feature is ready to ship. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-eval.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-evolve**: Cluster learned instincts into candidate commands, skills, and agents, and optionally generate those files. Use when instincts have accumulated and you want to promote repeated patterns into reusable structures. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-evolve.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-feature-start**: Kick off a feature: research context, create an isolated branch, confirm a green baseline, write a task breakdown, and the first failing test. Use when starting new feature work in a git repository. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-feature-start.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-fork**: Fork the current work into isolated git worktrees to try several approaches side by side, compare them on measured results, and merge the winner. Use when choosing between implementations, attempting a risky refactor, or testing competing bug theories. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-fork.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-harness-audit**: Run the deterministic harness audit and return a prioritized scorecard of the project's Claude Code setup: tools, context, quality gates, memory, evals, security, cost. Use when checking setup health or deciding what to harden next. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-harness-audit.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-improve**: Run the continuous-improvement pipeline: scan GitHub and npm for new Claude Code tooling, score proposals, and approve or reject them in a local queue. Use when reviewing ecosystem findings or deciding what to adopt next. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-improve.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-improve-commands**: Audit the slash command library: inventory every command, grade quality, find missing categories, and apply approved updates, additions, and archivals. Use when the command library feels stale, duplicated, or incomplete. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-improve-commands.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-init**: Scaffold or refresh a project's CLAUDE.md, tasks/, lessons.md and .claude/settings.json from the CC Commander template, preserving custom sections. Use when starting a project or when CLAUDE.md is missing, stale, or behind the template. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-init.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-instinct-export**: Export learned instincts from project, global or both scopes to a shareable YAML file, filtered by domain or confidence. Use when sharing instincts with teammates, moving them to another machine, or seeding project conventions. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-instinct-export.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-instinct-import**: Import instincts from a local YAML file or an HTTPS URL into project or global scope, previewing duplicates and confidence conflicts before anything is written. Use when adopting a teammate's or a project's exported instincts. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-instinct-import.md) · ★6)
- **ccc-instinct-status**: Show learned instincts for the current project and global scope, grouped by domain with confidence bars and observation stats. Use when checking what the continuous-learning system has picked up or before exporting or promoting instincts. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/blob/HEAD/commands/ccc-instinct-status.md) · ★6)

- **ceo-loop**: Activate the CEO Decision Loop in ORBIT mode. The Stop hook keeps the loop alive until the queue is empty or limits are reached. Usage: /nxtg-forge:ceo-loop [max-iterations] [time-limit-minutes] ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop.md) · ★7)

- **cf.del**: Deletes an item from a Cuckoo Filter ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cf.del.md) · ★86)

- **cfo-consolidar**: Consolidacao multi-entidade nos tres recortes de leitura — por entidade (visao individual), por grupo (soma das entidades eliminando transferencias intragrupo) e total consolidado (operador inteiro, com a fronteira PF-PJ sempre explicita). Base para holding e planejamento patrimonial. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-consolidar.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-contabil**: Triagem contabil — monta o pacote mensal para a contabilidade (extratos + notas + conciliacao do periodo), gera indice do pacote e relatorio de pendencias (o que falta, o que diverge) e empacota por competencia. NAO envia — o envio depende do gate de confirmacao em /cfo-enviar. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-contabil.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-credito**: MODULO SENSIVEL. Analisa emprestimos/financiamentos contra a taxa media oficial do Banco Central (busca ao vivo na API SGS, nunca chumba numero) e devolve municao comparativa — taxa contratada x media BCB x Selic, com semaforo calibrado juridicamente. Jamais afirma taxa ilegal ou abusiva; recomenda negociacao e remete a assessoria. Disclaimer fixo em toda saida. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-credito.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-dashboard**: Gera dashboard HTML single-file standalone (sem servidor, sem localStorage, dados injetados na geracao) com KPI cards semaforicos, fluxo de caixa com projecao e alerta de ruptura, aging AP/AR, benchmark de credito BCB com disclaimer fixo, saude de margem por produto e seletor de recorte multi-entidade (entidade/grupo/total). Responsivo, pronto para impressao/PDF. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-dashboard.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-enviar**: GATE DE ENVIO. Unica acao do plugin que toca conector externo (MCP Gmail/Outlook). Monta o e-mail para a contabilidade (destinatario do state, assunto, corpo, anexos) e SO dispara apos confirmacao explicita do operador. Sem confirmacao, instrui o envio manual. Nunca automatico, nunca agendado. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-enviar.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-full**: Pipeline completo de fechamento — ingere, concilia, analisa (fluxo de caixa + KPIs + provisoes), detecta anomalias, audita R1-R4, gera dashboard e monta o pacote de triagem contabil. PARA antes do envio (gate humano) — o disparo a contabilidade so acontece em /cfo-enviar com confirmacao explicita. Nunca envia automaticamente. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-full.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-ingest**: Importa dados financeiros brutos para o schema canonico de transacao — extratos bancarios/cartao (OFX/QFX/CSV/XLSX), NF-e/NFS-e (XML) e tabela de produtos/precos. Toda transacao recebe entidade + conta. Persistencia append-only por competencia, degradacao graciosa se a lib faltar. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-ingest.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-kpi**: Painel de KPIs de CFO — liquidez (corrente/seca/imediata), endividamento (geral, Divida Liq./EBITDA, cobertura de juros), prazos e ciclo (PMR/PMP/PME, ciclo financeiro), rentabilidade (margem bruta/EBITDA/liquida, ROI/ROE/ROA/ROIC) e operacional (break-even, giro, ticket medio). Cada KPI com leitura semaforica e comentario de CFO. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-kpi.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-pagar**: Contas a pagar — agenda de vencimentos com urgencia ponderada por valor (vencidas/hoje/7d/30d), fornecedores recorrentes e ordem otima de pagamento quando o caixa e insuficiente (custo de atraso x criticidade). ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-pagar.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-precos**: Benchmark de precos — valida preco vs custo vs margem, identifica margem negativa, abaixo do break-even ou fora da faixa da categoria, e gera ranking por saude de margem com simulacao de impacto de reprecificacao no resultado. Pesquisa de faixa de referencia de mercado so com autorizacao do operador. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-precos.md) · ★3)
- **cfo-receber**: Contas a receber — aging (a vencer / 1-30 / 31-60 / 61-90 / +90), ranqueia inadimplentes por valor x dias e gera fila de cobranca. ([sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/cfo-combativo-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/cfo-combativo-os/commands/cfo-receber.md) · ★3)
